## NEW — saved implementation, tests, and evidence

1. **Run546 failure diagnostic + safety-preserving test correction (PR #8):** preexisting CI [41386344362](https://github.com/Iam-2squared/ark-ai/actions/runs/41386344362) failed because the READ recovery validator correctly refused a swapped denial descriptor with message `recovery schedule altered before return`, whereas two test parametrizations expected only `descriptor changed`. 2. **V3 unapproved packet empty-key collision (PR #5; 9 authored cases):** `src/ark/learning/authorization_packet_delta_review.py` source `0694bb1a13036c352169b763cd3d0ff72656cf5e`, tests `tests/v3/test_packet_delta_empty_keys_run547.py` `d7b30655f100ff438fbcbe9592f274020c4d4572`. Exact tests HEAD [CI 41387525724](https://github.com/Iam-2squared/ark-ai/actions/runs/41387525724) **6/6 GREEN**.
3. **Synthetic READ result sealing (PR #8; 8 authored cases):** source `src/ark/agency/disconnected_result_budget.py` `7a3657e1c22569f8dc82b8d2e8422c62589c15e7`, tests `tests/test_detached_read_result_denial_epoch_run547.py` `a8492b6992d0b9947bf2606946225da1203c3188`. Only synthetic JSON/hash; no ToolBackend or authenticated result. Initial new-test CI failed lint; corrections noted above.
4. **V3 printable and non-spoofable packet paths (PR #5; 10 authored cases):** same module source `5b81bcbdbb712cd40d0b745c52e46f784763bd8c`, tests `tests/v3/test_packet_delta_printable_paths_run547.py` `30befe85d42bcfeed782dfd87e9b35fa8c5ae40b`. 5. **Memory expiry privacy denial (PR #8; 11 authored cases):** source `src/ark/memory/expiry_candidate_preview.py` `2b69e5d3b8ae6e0847d43ea3e0abe0cb9b274d96`, tests `tests/test_memory_expiry_descriptor_custody_run547.py` `6cc3777ed0164f5e3c0d183b5eeaf2e4ffef7a53`. ## NEW work — exact scope and limits

1. **Scheduler (7 new authored cases, PR #8):** `src/ark/offline_schedule_resume_review.py` reconstructs the pinned policy/cursor/time/occurrence/state/deadline fingerprint with the original domain-separated SHA-256 scheme rather than trusting the replaceable exact-class `DisconnectedDuePreview.fingerprint`. Claimed readiness is **only syntactic** and not proof of model/dataset/tool bytes, human review, license, billing approval or real Candidate/training authorization.
3. **Observation (9 new authored cases, PR #8):** `src/ark/offline_wave_energy_pipeline.py` parses the pinned original WAV `fmt(16)+data` header independently, reconciling exact reported sample rate, channels, PCM width, duration, declared bytes and pad before resampling. ## NEW work — exact implementation and limits

1. **Personal Context retention (10 authored cases, PR #8):** `src/ark/personal_context_retention_recheck_review.py` checks retained cached Memory/revocation source epochs, exact result metadata, unmodified nested current inventory, and denied-authority property descriptors *after each* reentrant getter. The archive and receipt journals remain **separate** reads, not a cross-store atomic transaction or authenticated backend effect; all real WRITE/UNKNOWN replay paths stay blocked.
3. **Scheduler restart (6 authored cases, PR #8):** `src/ark/offline_schedule_resume_review.py` independently derives the next synthetic occurrence identity, due time and state from pinned schedule policy/cursor and a **caller-asserted** clock. Forged exact-type preview callbacks, boolean index/type ambiguity and internally inconsistent due classification fail. No trusted clock, OS timer, real notification, automatic occurrence or replay is authorized.
4. **Personal Context request binding (8 authored cases, PR #8):** `src/ark/personal_context_request_binding.py` pins preview and binding permission property descriptors before reentrant callbacks, rechecking all source/result carriers and denied flags after each access. Equal-value digest replacement and rebindings of previously denied consent/export/retention/Tool properties cannot silently create authorization. No actual private content disclosed and no live revocation or consent source consulted.
5. **V3 passive transition (6 authored cases, PR #5):** `src/ark/learning/offline_readiness_transition.py` independently hashes canonical caller-supplied unapproved snapshot bytes to reject fabricated reviewer callback digests, freezes earlier shape-report fields and exact output carriers across last-denial callbacks. An untrusted local clock is NOT authenticated; no timer, event execution, notification, cursor advance, or automatic replay.
3. **Planner:** `src/ark/agency/disconnected_registered_archive_bridge.py` joins separately checked local mock-file SHA and complete registered READ receipt evidence for the exact bounded DAG and produces a domain-separated digest with explicit denied authority. This is **not** a cross-store atomic snapshot, external action result, or real READ/WRITE authorization. No ToolBackend calls; UNKNOWN WRITE remains manual-only. An invalid plan digest initially escaped as `DisconnectedBatchRejected`; the smallest semantics-preserving fix maps it to `RegisteredArchiveBridgeRejected`, with no weaker verification.

## NEW — scoped implementation, focused tests and known limitations

1. **Memory, 21 authored cases.** `src/ark/memory/retained_generation_fence.py` + `tests/test_memory_retained_generation_fence_run532.py`. Separately held `MemoryGenerationReview` is compared to a fresh complete bounded single-SQLite read; rejects event change/delete/recreate of the same deterministic ID even when revision 1 repeats, malformed type and false-value mutating authority getters. This does **not** make the frozen v1 numeric CAS write generation-safe or atomic, attest historical writer/custody, or authorize Memory mutation.
2. **Planner, 6 authored cases.** `src/ark/agency/disconnected_batch_terminal_projection.py` + `tests/test_disconnected_terminal_postdenial_run532.py`. Every terminal-result denied-authority callback is followed by exact report, class-descriptor and DAG/plan/schedule epoch checks. An inert hypothetical failed/blocked state is never treated as external execution. Four older test failures on the first source commit were a rejection-string mismatch; exact earlier `terminal receipt changed` message restored without relaxing rejection.
3. **Scheduler, 6 authored cases.** `src/ark/disconnected_schedule_preview.py` + `tests/test_schedule_preview_descriptor_epoch_run532.py`. A final false-returning consent getter cannot replace an earlier denied notification/Tool/replay/trusted-clock class descriptor. No timer, OS notification, trusted-clock assertion, real action or replay is started.
4. **V3, 16 authored cases.** `src/ark/learning/authorization_packet_delta_recheck.py` + `tests/v3/test_authorization_packet_delta_recheck_run532.py`. Revalidates two local unapproved packet files and returned exact canonical field-path delta against separately retained hashes; denies forged paths, drift, runtime authority and mutable-false denial callbacks. A new test initially omitted `execution_core_sha256` from the legitimate four-field change list; corrected the **test expectation only**, preserving authorization gates. No human approval, protected evaluation or Candidate execution.
5. **Privacy, 6 authored cases.** `src/ark/privacy_erasure_evidence_review.py` + `tests/test_privacy_erasure_descriptor_epoch_run532.py`. Pins all denied forensic-erasure / WAL-reclamation / cleanup property descriptors and rejects a final false property which swaps earlier or later descriptor. Even optimistic synthetic observations **never** prove real erasure, backup deletion or consent. No actual deletion.

No standalone local checkout/pytest/Ruff claimed. All executable writes used only the normal GitHub Contents API after an immediate latest-HEAD re-read; no force push, raw-object write, duplicate workflow dispatch or merge.

No new V3 recheck bypasses those gates. Memory v1's revision reuse still requires a separate generation-aware atomic CAS design before any actual write integration.

## NEW — source, focused tests, evidence

1. **Create-only detached mock batch archive (17 authored cases):** `src/ark/agency/disconnected_batch_cycle_archive.py`, `tests/test_disconnected_batch_cycle_archive_run531.py`. Stores only deterministic non-private fixture request/step digests, not raw arguments or results; bounded canonical JSON, exclusive file creation, fsync, non-aliased local regular file, independent retained file SHA and read-only restarts. Synthetic two-writer same-path race permits one winner; any ambiguous post-COMMIT file is left for manual review, never automatically deleted/retried. Returned witness grants no real dispatch/WRITE/replay/outcome authority. Initial source and source+test CI **FAILED Ruff E501**; `8a2c3779...` wrapped exactly the helper signature with no behavior change.
2. **V3 unapproved packet change paths (13 authored cases):** `src/ark/learning/authorization_packet_delta_review.py`, `tests/v3/test_authorization_packet_delta_review_run531.py`. Verifies two separately retained local packet paths and digests against two unapproved snapshots, then reports only bounded canonical changed field names/paths with a domain-separated SHA; no values or approvals. Two test cases correctly exposed an unwrapped existing `ExecutionBlocked` denial, mapped to a local `ReviewPacketDeltaRejected` without weakening any protected authorization gate; subsequent E501 exception-line wrap was lint-only.
3. **Memory CAS ABA lifetime warning (15 authored cases):** `src/ark/memory/cas_generation_review.py`, `tests/test_memory_cas_generation_aba_run531.py`. Diagnoses the fact that frozen Memory v1 can reuse revision 1 after a deterministic ID's deletion and recreation. Reconciles up to 40,000 complete SQLite event-lifetime rows plus current row in one read transaction; reports when a stale numeric CAS revision could now refer to a **different generation**, without altering frozen schema or claiming write, erasure, consent, or authenticated provenance. Includes restart with two handles, forged event sequences, old generation, bounds and read-only cases.

## NEW — source, tests, evidence

1. **V3 detached review packet (7 authored tests):** `src/ark/learning/authorization_packet_file_review.py` now pins decoded canonical JSON, returned witness hashes and exact non-authorizing `LocalPacketBindingWitness` type. 2. **Memory whole-store row/event time integrity (7 authored tests + 4 legacy expectation corrections):** `src/ark/memory/sqlite_event_alignment.py` now reconciles each live row's `created_at_ms` and `updated_at_ms` with the active CREATE/UPDATE event lifetime **in the same SQLite read transaction**. DELETE/EXPIRE then recreate resets the active creation time; caller clock need not be monotonic. Tests `tests/test_memory_event_timestamp_alignment_run530.py` include forged row/event timestamps, valid recreation and backward synthetic clock, plus coordinated unauthenticated rewrite changing a local digest. Correctly stronger rejection required four older parametrized tests in `tests/test_memory_alignment_full_row_digest_run508.py` and `tests/test_memory_retained_alignment_compare_run509.py` to expect fail-closed `MemoryEventAlignmentRejected` instead of an ordinary review digest. New-test-only errors (bad expected event count and undefined fixture variable) were also corrected without relaxing source checks.
3. **Planner/registered READ batch final-child epoch (2 authored tests):** `src/ark/agency/disconnected_registered_batch_receipt_set.py` pins the parent report's exact denial-property descriptors and rechecks non-authority **after** the last independent child-review pass. A reentrant final synthetic child callback cannot swap `backend_dispatched` to even another false-looking descriptor or a truthy one and still return a trusted report. Added two cases to `tests/test_registered_batch_receipt_set_run524.py`, verifying zero backend dispatch and no grant consumption. This does not turn independent SQLite READ journals into a shared atomic transaction, nor grant execution/replay.

**All 16 are AUTHORED pytest cases, not standalone local pytest PASS counts.** No direct local checkout/Ruff was claimed. All new source/test writes followed the normal allowed GitHub Contents path with HEAD re-read before writes. No hidden raw objects, force push or workflow rerun.

## NEW — source, tests and evidence

1. **Registered READ aggregate (9 cases):** `src/ark/agency/disconnected_registered_batch_receipt_set.py` independently recomputes the exact domain-separated child digest of a canonical step receipt, rather than trusting any lower-case 64-hex value from a replaceable child callback. It enforces exact string types on all child receipt scalar identifiers/hashes. A first source write accidentally encoded the digest domain NUL as literal backslash-zero; this was corrected at `b887a0e5c4b5f2bb8935d3a182a50a94d76fd242` before the complete test package, with no authority/dispatch relaxation. `tests/test_registered_batch_receipt_set_run524.py` adds forged valid-hex digests and same-value subclass cases. 2. **Privacy Context descriptor epoch (6 cases):** `src/ark/offline_context_membership_guard.py` retains the original four authority-property descriptor objects before result creation and rejects a false-valued final getter that reflectively changes an earlier authority property.
